1      *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
2      *&      Module  CANCEL_0100  INPUT
3      *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
4      *   To exit from program at any time
5      *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
6      MODULE cancel_0100 INPUT.
7        save_ok = ok_code.
8        CLEAR ok_code.
9        CASE save_ok.
10         WHEN 'BACK' OR 'CANCEL' OR 'EXIT'.
11           LEAVE PROGRAM.
12       ENDCASE.
13     ENDMODULE.                 " CANCEL_0100  INPUT

90     *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
91     *&      Module  USER_COMMAND_0100  INPUT
92     *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
93     * To call screen 200.
94     * Lock the record(s) in case of change/delete before calling screen 0200
95     *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
96     MODULE user_command_0100 INPUT.
97       g_ok_code = ok_code.
98       CLEAR ok_code.
99
100    *+002 : Start
101      PERFORM chk_authority USING sy-uname
102                                  g_ok_code.
103    *+002 : End
104
105      CASE g_ok_code.
106
107        WHEN 'NEW'.
108          g_tcode = 'ZMMTDR1'.
109
110          CALL SCREEN '0200'.
111
112        WHEN 'CHNG'.
113
114          g_tcode = 'ZMMTDR2'.
115
116          CALL SCREEN '0150'.
117
118        WHEN 'DISP'.
119
120          g_tcode = 'ZMMTDR3'.
121
122          g_dmode = 'X'.
123          g_dmode_spfc = 'X'.
124
125          g_dmode_epc = 'X'.                                    "+001
126
127          CALL SCREEN '0150'.
128
129    *+002 : Start
130        WHEN 'APRV'.
131
132          g_tcode = 'ZMMTDR3'.
133
134          g_dmode = 'X'.
135          g_dmode_spfc = 'X'.
136
137          g_dmode_epc = 'X'.
138

867          EXPORTING

981                      submi = zmm_pur_tender_d_st-submi.

997    *    MESSAGE ID sy-msgid TYPE sy-msgty NUMBER sy-msgno
998    *           WITH sy-msgv1 sy-msgv2 sy-msgv3 sy-msgv4.

1858   *+006 : Start

1868          g_ok_code_300 NE 'PCHK'.
1869   *+006 : End
1870   *   IF zmm_pur_tender_d_st-banfn IS INITIAL.            "-006
1871   *     IF g_no_pr <> 'X'.                                "-006
1872         IF zmm_pur_tender_d_st-prchk NE 'X'.              "+006
1873           MESSAGE e321(zmm) WITH text-015 .
1874         ENDIF.
1875       ENDIF.
1876     ENDIF.

1878   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
1879   *&      Module  CHECK_INITIAL  INPUT
1880   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
1881   *       text
1882   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
1883   MODULE check_initial INPUT.
1884   *  data : l_bstyp TYPE zmm_pur_trtyp-bstyp.
1885   *  clear : l_bstyp.
1886   *
1887   *  select single BSTYP
1888   *    into l_bstyp
1889   *    from ZMM_PUR_TRTYP
1890   *    where BSART = ZMM_PUR_TENDER_D_ST-BSART.
1891   *  if sy-subrc = 0.
1892   *    if l_bstyp = 'L'.
1893   *    else.
1894   *      MESSAGE e321(zmm) WITH text-016 .
1895   *    endif.
1896   *  endif.

82           SET TITLEBAR 'T20C'.
83
84         WHEN 'DISP'.
85           REFRESH ist_pf_status.
86           MOVE 'SAVE' TO wa_pf_status-fcode.
87           APPEND wa_pf_status TO ist_pf_status.
88
89           MOVE 'ATCH' TO wa_pf_status-fcode.
90           APPEND wa_pf_status TO ist_pf_status.
91
92           SET PF-STATUS 'S200' EXCLUDING ist_pf_status.
93           SET TITLEBAR 'T20D'.
94
95     *+002 : Start
96         WHEN 'APRV'.
97
98           REFRESH ist_pf_status.
99           MOVE 'LIST' TO wa_pf_status-fcode.
100          APPEND wa_pf_status TO ist_pf_status.
101
102          MOVE 'ATCH' TO wa_pf_status-fcode.
103          APPEND wa_pf_status TO ist_pf_status.
104
105          SET PF-STATUS 'S200' EXCLUDING ist_pf_status.
106          SET TITLEBAR 'T20A'.
107    *+002 : End
108
109      ENDCASE.

727              CLEAR g_dmode_spfc.

731                screen-output  = '1'.

733              ENDIF.

735            ELSE.

738

740                screen-input   = '1'.
741                screen-output  = '1'.
742                MODIFY SCREEN.
743              ENDIF.
744
745            ENDIF.
746
747          ENDLOOP.
748
749    *   WHEN 'DISP'.                                            "-002
750        WHEN 'DISP' OR 'APRV'.                                  "+002
751          LOOP AT SCREEN.
752            IF screen-group2 = 'MET'.
753              screen-input   = '0'.
754              screen-output  = '0'.
755              MODIFY SCREEN.
756            ENDIF.

758      ENDCASE.

1279   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
1280   *&      Module  DEFAULT_PR_PO_REL_DT  OUTPUT
1281   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
1282   *       text
1283   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
1284   MODULE default_pr_po_rel_dt OUTPUT.
1285
1286   *In table CDPOS, Put  OBJECTCLAS- BANF OBJECTID- PR NumberFNAME- FRGKZ
1287   *Execute.
1288   *Take lowest CHANGENR and put it in table CDHDR in field CHANGENR.
1289   *Also enter OBJECTCLAS- BANF, OBJECTID- PR Number and get UDATE.
1290   *-007 : Start
1291   *  DATA : ist_cdhdr TYPE TABLE OF cdhdr,
1292   *         ist_cdpos TYPE TABLE OF cdpos,
1293   *         ist_cdpos_po TYPE TABLE OF cdpos,
1294   *         wa_cdpos TYPE cdpos,
1295   *         wa_cdhdr TYPE cdhdr,
1296   *         l_ebeln TYPE eban-ebeln.
1297   *
1298   *  CLEAR : ist_cdhdr ,ist_cdpos,l_ebeln.
1299   *  REFRESH : ist_cdhdr ,ist_cdpos.
1300   *-007 : End
1301
1302   *+007 : Start
1303     DATA : l_ebeln TYPE eban-ebeln.
1304   *+007 : End
1305
1306     PERFORM read_change_doc USING 'BANF'  zmm_pur_tender_d_st-banfn  'FRGKZ'
1307           CHANGING zmm_tms_general-pr_ff_rel_dt.
1308
1309     SELECT EBELN FROM EBAN
1310    INTO L_EBELN UP TO 1 ROWS WHERE BANFN = ZMM_PUR_TENDER_D_ST-BANFN
1311    ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY .
1312    ENDSELECT.
1313
1314     SELECT SINGLE SUBMI from ekko INTO @data(v_SUBMI)
1315       WHERE ebeln = @l_ebeln.
1316
1317       IF v_SUBMI Ne zmm_pur_tender_d_st-submi .
1318       clear l_ebeln.
1319       ENDIF.
1320
1321
1322     PERFORM read_change_doc USING 'EINKBELEG'  l_ebeln  'FRGKE'
1323           CHANGING zmm_tms_general-po_ff_rel_dt.
1324   *  DATA : BEGIN  OF ist_editpos OCCURS 0 .
1325   *          INCLUDE STRUCTURE cdshw .
1326   *  DATA :  user LIKE sy-uname ,
1327   *          date LIKE sy-datum ,
1328   *  END OF ist_editpos .
1329   *  DATA :   i_editpos TYPE TABLE OF cdshw WITH HEADER LINE .
1330   *  DATA : wa_header LIKE cdhdr .
1331   *  objectclass = 'BANF'.
1332   *  objectid = ZMM_PUR_TENDER_D_ST-BANFN.
1333   *  CALL FUNCTION 'CHANGEDOCUMENT_READ_HEADERS'
1334   *    EXPORTING
1335   *      objectclass = objectclass
1336   *      objectid    = objectid
1337   *      username    = space
1338   *    TABLES
1339   *      i_cdhdr     = tcdhdr
1340   *    EXCEPTIONS
1341   *      OTHERS      = 1.
1342   *
1343   *  IF sy-subrc =  0.
1344   *
1345   *    SORT tcdhdr asCENDING BY changenr.
1346   *    READ TABLE tcdhdr index 1.
1347   **    LOOP AT  tcdhdr .
1348   *    CLEAR : i_editpos, ist_editpos , wa_header .
1349   *    REFRESH i_editpos .
1350   *    CALL FUNCTION 'CHANGEDOCUMENT_READ_POSITIONS'
1351   *      EXPORTING
1352   *        changenumber            = tcdhdr-changenr
1353   *      IMPORTING
1354   *        header                  = wa_header
1355   *      TABLES
1356   *        editpos                 = i_editpos
1357   *      EXCEPTIONS
1358   *        no_position_found       = 1
1359   *        wrong_access_to_archive = 2
1360   *        OTHERS                  = 3.
1361   *    IF sy-subrc =  0.
1362   *      LOOP AT i_editpos WHERE fname = 'FRGKZ' .
1363   **          MOVE-CORRESPONDING i_editpos TO ist_editpos.
1364   **          ist_editpos-user = wa_header-username .
1365   **          ist_editpos-date = wa_header-udate .
1366   **          APPEND ist_editpos .
1367   *        ZMM_TMS_GENERAL-PR_FF_REL_DT = ist_cdhdr-udate.
1368   *      ENDLOOP.
1369   *    ENDIF.
1370   **    ENDLOOP .
1371   *  endif.
1372   *  SORT tcdhdr asCENDING BY changenr.
1373   *  READ TABLE tcdhdr index 1.
1374   *data : ist_cdpos TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF cdpos.
1375   *
1376   *    SELECT * FROM cdpos
1377   *          INTO TABLE ist_cdpos
1378   *          WHERE objectclas EQ 'BANF' AND
1379   **                tabname EQ 'EKKO' AND
1380   *                objectid EQ tcdhdr-objectid AND
1381   *                changenr EQ tcdhdr-changenr AND
1382   *                fname EQ ' FRGKZ'.
1383
1384   *  ZMM_TMS_GENERAL-PR_FF_REL_DT = tcdhdr-udate.
1385   *  read table tcdhdr index 1.
1386   *  CALL FUNCTION 'CHANGEDOCUMENT_READ_POSITIONS'
1387   *    EXPORTING
1388   *      changenumber = tcdhdr-changenr
1389   *    TABLES
1390   *      editpos      = lt_cdshw
1391   *    EXCEPTIONS
1392   *      OTHERS       = 1.
1393   *
1394   *  LOOP AT lt_cdshw.
1395   *    IF lt_cdshw-fname EQ 'FRGKZ'.
1396   *
1397   *    endif.
1398   *  endloop.
1399   *  if not ZMM_PUR_TENDER_D_ST-SUBMI is initial.
1400   **  ZMM_TMS_GENERAL-PO_FF_REL_DT
1401   *    select single * from ekko
1402   *    where submi = ZMM_PUR_TENDER_D_ST-SUBMI
1403   *    and ebeln = '4010009865'.
1404   *
1405   *
1406   *    DATA: BEGIN OF ICDHDR OCCURS 50.
1407   *            INCLUDE STRUCTURE CDHDR.
1408   *    DATA: END OF ICDHDR.
1409   *
1410   *    DATA: BEGIN OF ICDSHW OCCURS 50.
1411   *            INCLUDE STRUCTURE CDSHW.
1412   *    DATA: END OF ICDSHW.
1413   *
1414   *    DATA: BEGIN OF ITAB OCCURS 50,
1415   *            BEGIN OF EKKEY,
1416   *              EBELN LIKE EKET-EBELN,
1417   *              EBELP LIKE EKET-EBELP,
1418   *
1419   *              ETENR LIKE EKET-ETENR,
1420   *            END OF EKKEY,
1421   *            CHANGENR LIKE CDHDR-CHANGENR,
1422   *            UDATE    LIKE CDHDR-UDATE,
1423   *            UTIME    LIKE CDHDR-UTIME,
1424   *            USERNAME LIKE CDHDR-USERNAME,
1425   *            CHNGIND  LIKE CDSHW-CHNGIND,
1426   *            FTEXT    LIKE CDSHW-FTEXT,
1427   *            OUTLEN   LIKE CDSHW-OUTLEN,
1428   *            F_OLD    LIKE CDSHW-F_OLD,
1429   *            F_NEW    LIKE CDSHW-F_NEW,
1430   *          END OF ITAB.
1431   *    CLEAR CDHDR.

440    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
441    *  -->  p_submi   Tender number
442    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
443    FORM lock_record USING p_submi.
444
445      CALL FUNCTION 'ENQUEUE_EZ_SUBMI'
446        EXPORTING
447          mode_zmm_pur_tender_d = 'E'
448          mandt                 = sy-mandt
449          submi                 = p_submi
450        EXCEPTIONS
451          foreign_lock          = 1
452          system_failure        = 2
453          OTHERS                = 3.
454

456    *    MESSAGE ID sy-msgid TYPE sy-msgty NUMBER sy-msgno
457    *           WITH sy-msgv1 sy-msgv2 sy-msgv3 sy-msgv4.
458      ENDIF.
459
460    ENDFORM.                    " lock_record

472        EXPORTING
473          mode_zmm_pur_tender_d = 'E'
474          mandt                 = sy-mandt
475          submi                 = p_submi.
476

563    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
564    *&      Form  generate_tender_number
565    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
566    *       text
567    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
568    *  -->  p1        text
569    *  <--  p2        text
570    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
571    FORM generate_tender_number.
572
573      DATA : l_number(3) TYPE n,
574             lsubmi      LIKE zmm_pur_tender_d_st-srno.
575
576      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zmm_pur_trtyp WHERE bsart EQ
577                                               zmm_pur_tender_d_st-bsart.
578
579      SELECT MAX( srno )  INTO lsubmi FROM zmm_pur_tender_d
580                           WHERE ekgrp     EQ zmm_pur_tender_d_st-ekgrp
581                           AND   dlg_offic EQ zmm_pur_tender_d_st-dlg_offic
582                           AND   mjahr     EQ zmm_pur_tender_d_st-mjahr.
583
584      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
585        l_number = '001'.
586      ELSEIF sy-subrc EQ 0.
587        l_number = lsubmi + 1.
588      ENDIF.
589
590      zmm_pur_tender_d_st-srno = l_number.
591
592      CONCATENATE zmm_pur_tender_d_st-ekgrp
593                  zmm_pur_tender_d_st-dlg_offic
594                  zmm_pur_trtyp-bstyp
595                  zmm_pur_tender_d_st-mjahr+2(2)
596                  l_number INTO zmm_pur_tender_d_st-submi.
597
598
599    ENDFORM.                    " generate_tender_number

894    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
895    *&      Form  oif_conv_date_time_ts
896    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
897    *       text
898    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
899    *  -->  p1        text
900    *  <--  p2        text
901    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
902    FORM oif_conv_date_time_ts.
903      DATA l_timestamp LIKE oifbbp1-ftmstm.
904
905      CALL FUNCTION 'OIF_CONV_DATE_TIME_TS'
906        EXPORTING
907          i_datum           = sy-datum
908          i_time            = sy-uzeit
909        IMPORTING
910          e_timestamp       = l_timestamp
911        EXCEPTIONS
912          date_not_received = 1
913          time_not_received = 2
914          OTHERS            = 3.
915
916      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
917    *    MESSAGE ID sy-msgid TYPE sy-msgty NUMBER sy-msgno
918    *            WITH sy-msgv1 sy-msgv2 sy-msgv3 sy-msgv4.
919      ENDIF.
920      zmm_pur_tender_d_st-timestamp = l_timestamp.
921
922    ENDFORM.                    " oif_conv_date_time_ts
923    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
924    *&      Form  validate_date_req
925    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
926    *       text
927    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
928    *  -->  p1        text
929    *  <--  p2        text
930    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
931    FORM validate_date_req.
932
933      CHECK zmm_pur_tender_d_st-bsart+0(1) NE 'E'.
934
935      IF zmm_pur_tender_d_st-tdrdt IS INITIAL.
936        MESSAGE e027(zmmpurtdr).
937      ENDIF.
938
939      IF g_ok_code NE 'NEW'.                         "+008
940        IF zmm_pur_tender_d_st-bsart+0(2) EQ 'MO'  OR
941           zmm_pur_tender_d_st-bsart      EQ 'SGO' OR
942           zmm_pur_tender_d_st-bsart EQ 'SOT'      OR
943           zmm_pur_tender_d_st-bsart EQ 'MRCO'.
944
945          """""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""
946          "comment by lipsy  on 5.11.2014 FOR new validations in RD1K994950
947    *      IF zmm_pur_tender_d_st-st_sel_dt IS INITIAL OR
948    *         zmm_pur_tender_d_st-lt_sel_dt IS INITIAL.
949    *
950    *
951    *        PERFORM err_msg USING '008'.
952    *
953    *        endif.
954
955          "end of comment by lipsy on 5.11.2014  FOR new validations in RD1K994950
956          """"""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""
957
958
959          """"""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""
960          "add by lipsy on 5.11.2014 FOR new validations in RD1K994950
961
962          IF zmm_pur_tender_d_st-st_sel_dt IS INITIAL
963           AND  zmm_pur_tender_d_st-sch_st_sel_dt < sy-datum.
964            PERFORM err_msg USING '008'.
965          ENDIF.
966

968          IF     zmm_pur_tender_d_st-lt_sel_dt IS INITIAL
969           AND zmm_pur_tender_d_st-sch_lt_sel_dt < sy-datum.
970            PERFORM err_msg USING '038'.

973            """""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""
974          ENDIF.
975        ENDIF.

978        "added by lipsy on 1.04.2015 ""RD1K995870
979        IF  zmm_tms_pb-pr_bid_op_act_dt IS NOT INITIAL AND zmm_tms_tb-tend_op_act_dt IS NOT INITIAL.
980          IF  zmm_tms_pb-pr_bid_op_act_dt LT zmm_tms_tb-tend_op_act_dt.
981            PERFORM err_msg USING '040'.
982          ENDIF.
983        ENDIF.

986          IF zmm_tms_pb-loi_1_act_dt LT zmm_tms_pb-pr_bid_op_act_dt.
987            PERFORM err_msg USING '041'.
988          ENDIF.
989        ENDIF.
990        "end of addition by lipsy on 1.04.2015 ""RD1K995870

992        """""""""""""
993
994
995      ENDIF.                                           "+008

998      IF zmm_pur_tender_d_st-bsart = 'MLLM' OR
999         zmm_pur_tender_d_st-bsart = 'MLLN' OR
1000        zmm_pur_tender_d_st-bsart = 'MLMM' OR
1001        zmm_pur_tender_d_st-bsart = 'MLMN' OR
1002        zmm_pur_tender_d_st-bsart = 'MRCM' OR

1005        zmm_pur_tender_d_st-bsart = 'SBP'  OR
1006        zmm_pur_tender_d_st-bsart = 'SGL'  OR
1007        zmm_pur_tender_d_st-bsart = 'SLT'  OR
1008        zmm_pur_tender_d_st-bsart = 'SST'.
1009
1010       FIELD-SYMBOLS: <f1>.
1011       FIELD-SYMBOLS: <f2>.
1012
1013       DATA : tabn(20)   TYPE c,
1014              fieldn(15) TYPE c,
1015              rc.
1016       tabn = 'ZMM_PUR_TENDER_D'.
1017
1018       LOOP AT SCREEN.
1019         CHECK screen-group2 EQ 'DSP'.
1020         ASSIGN (screen-name) TO <f1>.
1021         IF NOT <f1> IS INITIAL.
1022           SEARCH screen-name FOR '-'.
1023           sy-fdpos = sy-fdpos + 1.
1024           ASSIGN screen-name+sy-fdpos(10) TO <f2>.
1025           MOVE <f2> TO fieldn.
1026           PERFORM get_ftext(rddfie00) USING tabn fieldn sy-langu
1027                                       CHANGING dfies rc.
1028           IF rc EQ 0.

1030             MESSAGE e024(zmmpurtdr) WITH dfies-scrtext_m
1031                                          zmm_pur_tender_d_st-bsart.
1032           ENDIF.

1399              lttyp = 'M'.
1400

1496   *      -->P_ACT_DT_FN  Actual date : Dynpro field name
1497   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
1498   FORM chk_sch_act_date  USING    p_sch_dt
1499                                   p_act_dt
1500                                   p_sch_dt_fn
1501                                   p_act_dt_fn.
1502
1503   ************************************************************************
1504   *  IF NOT p_sch_dt IS INITIAL.
1505   *
1506   *    IF p_sch_dt GT sy-datum.
1507   *
1508   *      SET CURSOR FIELD p_sch_dt_fn.
1509   *
1510   *      MESSAGE e028(zmmpurtdr).
1511   *
1512   *    ENDIF.
1513   *
1514   *    IF NOT p_act_dt IS INITIAL.
1515   *
1516   *      IF p_act_dt LT p_sch_dt.
1517   *
1518   *        SET CURSOR FIELD p_act_dt_fn.
1519   *
1520   *        MESSAGE e030(zmmpurtdr).
1521   *
1522   *      ENDIF.
1523   *
1524   *    ENDIF.
1525   *
1526   *  ELSE.
1527   *
1528   *    IF NOT p_act_dt IS INITIAL.
1529   *
1530   *      SET CURSOR FIELD p_sch_dt_fn.
1531   *
1532   *      MESSAGE e029(zmmpurtdr).
1533   *
1534   *    ENDIF.
1535   *
1536   *  ENDIF.
1537   ***********************************************************************
1538
1539   ***********************************************************************
1540     IF NOT p_act_dt IS INITIAL.
1541
1542       IF p_act_dt GT sy-datum.
1543
1544         SET CURSOR FIELD p_act_dt_fn.
1545
1546         """"""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""
1547         """ADDED BY LIPSY ON 30.10.2014  FOR  removing error in display mode RD1K994950
1548         IF g_ok_code = 'DISP'.
1549
1550         ELSE.
1551
1552           "END OF ADDITION BY LIPSY ON 30.10.2014 FOR removing error in display mode RD1K994950
1553           """"""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""
1554           MESSAGE e031(zmmpurtdr).
1555
1556
1557           """""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""
1558           """ADDED BY LIPSY ON 30.10.2014  FOR removing error in display mode RD1K994950
1559
1560         ENDIF.
1561         "END OF ADDITION BY LIPSY ON 30.10.2014 FOR removing error in display mode RD1K994950
1562         """""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""
1563       ENDIF.
1564
1565       IF p_sch_dt IS INITIAL.
1566
1567         SET CURSOR FIELD p_sch_dt_fn.
1568
1569         """"""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""
1570         """ADDED BY LIPSY ON 30.10.2014  FOR  removing error in display mode RD1K994950
1571         IF g_ok_code = 'DISP'.
1572
1573         ELSE.
1574
1575           "END OF ADDITION BY LIPSY ON 30.10.2014 FOR removing error in display mode RD1K994950
1576

1895   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
1896   FORM chk_authority  USING    p_uname
1897                                p_sycod.
1898
1899     DATA : l_agr_name TYPE agr_users-agr_name.
1900     IF p_uname+0(1) <> 'C'.
1901       IF ( p_sycod = 'DISP' OR p_sycod = 'REP' ).
1902         SELECT AGR_NAME FROM AGR_USERS INTO L_AGR_NAME UP TO 1 ROWS
1903    WHERE ( AGR_NAME = TEXT-017 ) AND UNAME = P_UNAME
1904    ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY .
1905    ENDSELECT.
1906
1907         IF sy-subrc NE 0 AND l_agr_name IS INITIAL.
1908
1909           CASE p_sycod.
1910             WHEN 'NEW'.
1911               MESSAGE e043(zpm) WITH text-009.
1912             WHEN 'CHNG'.
1913               MESSAGE e043(zpm) WITH text-010.
1914             WHEN 'DISP'.
1915               MESSAGE e043(zpm) WITH text-011.
1916             WHEN 'APRV'.
1917               MESSAGE e043(zpm) WITH text-012.
1918           ENDCASE.
1919
1920         ENDIF.
1921       ELSE.
1922         SELECT AGR_NAME FROM AGR_USERS INTO L_AGR_NAME UP TO 1 ROWS
1923    WHERE ( AGR_NAME = TEXT-007 OR AGR_NAME = TEXT-008 ) AND UNAME = P_UNAME
1924    ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY .
1925    ENDSELECT.
1926
1927         IF sy-subrc NE 0 AND l_agr_name IS INITIAL.
1928
1929           CASE p_sycod.
1930             WHEN 'NEW'.
1931               MESSAGE e043(zpm) WITH text-009.
1932             WHEN 'CHNG'.
1933               MESSAGE e043(zpm) WITH text-010.
1934             WHEN 'DISP'.
1935               MESSAGE e043(zpm) WITH text-011.
1936             WHEN 'APRV'.
1937               MESSAGE e043(zpm) WITH text-012.
1938           ENDCASE.
1939
1940         ENDIF.
1941       ENDIF.
1942     ENDIF.
1943   ENDFORM.                    " CHK_AUTHORITY

2216   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
2217   *&      Form  READ_CHANGE_DOC
2218   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
2219   *       text
2220   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
2221   *      -->P_1400   text
2222   *      -->P_ZMM_PUR_TENDER_D_ST_BANFN  text
2223   *      -->P_1402   text
2224   *      <--P_ZMM_TMS_GENERAL_PR_FF_REL_DT  text
2225   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
2226   FORM read_change_doc  USING  p_class
2227                                p_objectid
2228                                p_fname
2229                         CHANGING p_zdate.
2230   *+007 : Start
2231     DATA : ist_cdhdr    TYPE TABLE OF cdhdr,
2232            ist_cdpos    TYPE TABLE OF cdpos,
2233            ist_cdpos_po TYPE TABLE OF cdpos,
2234            wa_cdpos     TYPE cdpos,
2235            wa_cdhdr     TYPE cdhdr.
2236   *+007 : End
2237
2238     CLEAR : ist_cdpos, wa_cdpos,ist_cdhdr,wa_cdhdr.
2239     REFRESH : ist_cdpos, ist_cdhdr.
2240     SELECT * FROM cdpos
2241       INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE ist_cdpos
2242       WHERE   objectclas EQ p_class AND
2243                    objectid EQ p_objectid AND
2244                     fname EQ p_fname ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY.
2245     SORT ist_cdpos ASCENDING BY changenr.
2246     IF p_fname = 'FRGKE'.
2247       LOOP AT ist_cdpos INTO wa_cdpos
2248         WHERE value_new = 'S'.
2249         APPEND wa_cdpos TO ist_cdpos_po.
2250       ENDLOOP.
2251       ist_cdpos[] = ist_cdpos_po[].
2252       SORT ist_cdpos ASCENDING BY changenr.
2253     ENDIF.
2254     READ TABLE ist_cdpos INTO wa_cdpos INDEX 1.
2255     SELECT * FROM cdhdr
2256     INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE ist_cdhdr
2257     WHERE   objectclas EQ  p_class AND
2258                  objectid EQ p_objectid AND
2259                   changenr EQ wa_cdpos-changenr.
2260
2261
2262     READ TABLE ist_cdhdr INTO wa_cdhdr INDEX 1.
2263     p_zdate = wa_cdhdr-udate.
2264   ENDFORM.                    " READ_CHANGE_DOC

2848   *&      Form  SET_PRE_BID_DT
2849   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
2850   * To calculate scheduled date
2851   *  - Receipt of querry from Bidders
2852   *  - Scrutiny and PBC Date
2853   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
2854   *      -->P_PBC_STAT         Pre bid conference status
2855   *      -->P_ST_SCH_LT_SEL    Schedule last date of selling tender
2856   *      -->P_LSTK             LSTK Project type
2857   *      <--P_RCPT_BID_SCH_DT  Date of receipt of querry from Bidders
2858   *      <--P_PBC_SCH_DT       Pre bid conference date
2859   *      <--P_TC_MET_PB_SCH_DT Date of approval of TC meeting - Prebid ...
2860   *      <--P_REO_TSAL_STAT    Reopening of sale required
2861   *      <--P_AMD_ISSU_SCH_DT  Date of Issue of amendments after PBC
2862   *      <--P_REO_TSAL_SCH_DT  Re-Opening of tender sale
2863   *      <--P_REC_TSAL_SCH_DT  Re-Closing of tender sale
2864   *      <-P_SUBM_DL_SCH_DT    Submission Deadline of Tender
2865   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
2866   FORM set_pre_bid_dt  USING    p_pbc_stat
2867                                 p_st_sch_lt_sel
2868                                 p_lstk
2869                        CHANGING p_rcpt_bid_sch_dt
2870                                 p_pbc_sch_dt
2871                                 p_tc_met_pb_sch_dt
2872                                 p_reo_tsal_stat
2873                                 p_amd_issu_sch_dt
2874                                 p_reo_tsal_sch_dt
2875                                 p_rec_tsal_sch_dt
2876                                 p_subm_dl_sch_dt.
2877
2878     IF p_pbc_stat = 'Y' AND NOT p_st_sch_lt_sel IS INITIAL.
2879
2880   *Scheduled date of receipt of querry from Bidders
2881       p_rcpt_bid_sch_dt = p_st_sch_lt_sel + 7.
2882
2883   *Scheduled scrutiny and PBC Date
2884       p_pbc_sch_dt = p_rcpt_bid_sch_dt + 8.
2885
2886   *Scheduled date of approval of TC meeting - Prebid confer. Issue
2887       p_tc_met_pb_sch_dt = p_pbc_sch_dt + 8.
2888
2889       IF p_reo_tsal_stat = 'Y'.
2890   *Date of Issue of amendments after PBC
2891         p_amd_issu_sch_dt = p_pbc_sch_dt + 13.
2892
2893   *Re-Opening of tender sale
2894   *     p_reo_tsal_sch_dt = p_amd_issu_sch_dt.
2895         p_reo_tsal_sch_dt = p_amd_issu_sch_dt + 7.
2896
2897   *Re-Closing of tender sale
2898   *     p_rec_tsal_sch_dt = p_amd_issu_sch_dt + 15.
2899         p_rec_tsal_sch_dt = p_reo_tsal_sch_dt + 15.
2900
2901       ELSE.
2902   *Date of Issue of amendments after PBC
2903         p_amd_issu_sch_dt = p_pbc_sch_dt + 8.
2904
2905         CLEAR : p_reo_tsal_sch_dt,
2906                 p_rec_tsal_sch_dt.
2907       ENDIF.
2908
2909     ELSE.
2910
2911       CLEAR : p_rcpt_bid_sch_dt,
2912               p_pbc_sch_dt,
2913               p_tc_met_pb_sch_dt,
2914               p_amd_issu_sch_dt,
2915               p_reo_tsal_sch_dt,
2916               p_rec_tsal_sch_dt.
2917
2918   *    p_reo_tsal_stat = 'N'.
2919
2920     ENDIF.
2921
2922     IF NOT zmm_pur_tender_d_st-sch_lt_sel_dt IS INITIAL OR
2923        NOT p_amd_issu_sch_dt IS INITIAL OR
2924        NOT p_rec_tsal_sch_dt IS INITIAL.
2925
2926       PERFORM calc_subm_deadline USING p_pbc_stat
2927                                        p_lstk
2928                                        p_reo_tsal_stat
2929                                        zmm_pur_tender_d_st-sch_lt_sel_dt
2930                                        p_amd_issu_sch_dt
2931                                        p_rec_tsal_sch_dt
2932                                    CHANGING p_subm_dl_sch_dt.
2933     ENDIF.
2934
2935   ENDFORM.                    " SET_PRE_BID_DT

3108   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3109   *&      Form  SET_PRE_BID_L_DT
3110   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3111   * To calculate scheduled date
3112   *  - Receipt of querry from Bidders
3113   *  - Scrutiny and PBC Date
3114   *  -
3115   *  -
3116   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
3117   *      -->P_PBC_STAT         Pre bid conference status
3118   *      -->P_TNDR_VAL         Tender value
3119   *      -->P_SCH_TDRDT        Schedule tender date
3120   *      -->P_VERF_ISS_SCH_DT  Scheduled verification & issue of tender..
3121   *      -->P_LSTK             LSTK Project type
3122   *      <--P_RCPT_BID_SCH_DT  Date of receipt of querry from Bidders
3123   *      <--P_PBC_SCH_DT       Pre bid conference date
3124   *      <--P_AMD_ISSU_SCH_DT  Date of Issue of amendments after PBC
3125   *      <-P_SUBM_DL_SCH_DT    Submission Deadline of Tender
3126   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
3127   FORM set_pre_bid_l_dt  USING    p_pbc_stat
3128                                   p_tndr_val
3129                                   p_sch_tdrdt
3130                                   p_verf_iss_sch_dt
3131                                   p_lstk
3132                          CHANGING p_rcpt_bid_sch_dt
3133                                   p_pbc_sch_dt
3134                                   p_amd_issu_sch_dt
3135                                   p_subm_dl_sch_dt.
3136
3137     DATA : l_tval_stat(1),
3138            l_reo_tsal_stat(1) VALUE 'N',
3139            l_date             TYPE sy-datum.
3140
3141   *Tender Value Status
3142     IF p_tndr_val GT 2500000.
3143
3144       l_tval_stat = 'A'.
3145
3146     ELSEIF p_tndr_val BETWEEN 500000 AND 2500000.
3147
3148       l_tval_stat = 'B'.
3149
3150     ELSE.
3151
3152       l_tval_stat = 'C'.
3153
3154     ENDIF.
3155
3156     IF p_pbc_stat = 'Y' AND NOT p_verf_iss_sch_dt IS INITIAL.
3157
3158       IF l_tval_stat = 'A'.
3159
3160   *Scheduled date of receipt of querry from Bidders
3161         p_rcpt_bid_sch_dt = p_verf_iss_sch_dt + 7.
3162
3163   *Scheduled scrutiny and PBC Date
3164         p_pbc_sch_dt = p_rcpt_bid_sch_dt + 8.
3165
3166   *Date of Issue of amendments after PBC[Approval & Issue of PBC Minutes]
3167         p_amd_issu_sch_dt = p_pbc_sch_dt + 8.
3168
3169       ELSE.
3170
3171         CLEAR : p_rcpt_bid_sch_dt,
3172                 p_pbc_sch_dt,
3173                 p_amd_issu_sch_dt.
3174
3175       ENDIF.
3176
3177     ENDIF.
3178
3179     IF l_tval_stat = 'A'.
3180
3181       IF NOT p_verf_iss_sch_dt IS INITIAL OR
3182          NOT  p_amd_issu_sch_dt IS INITIAL.
3183
3184         PERFORM calc_subm_deadline USING p_pbc_stat
3185                                          p_lstk
3186                                          l_reo_tsal_stat
3187                                          p_verf_iss_sch_dt
3188                                          p_amd_issu_sch_dt
3189                                          l_date
3190                                       CHANGING p_subm_dl_sch_dt.
3191
3192       ENDIF.
3193
3194     ELSEIF l_tval_stat = 'B'.
3195
3196       p_subm_dl_sch_dt = p_verf_iss_sch_dt + 10.
3197
3198     ELSEIF l_tval_stat = 'C'.
3199
3200       p_subm_dl_sch_dt = p_sch_tdrdt + 21.
3201
3202     ENDIF.
3203
3204   ENDFORM.                    " SET_PRE_BID_L_DT

3245         upd_zmm_tms             = 'U'

3251   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3252   *&      Form  GET_NO_RFQ_MAINT
3253   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3254   * To get Nos. of RFQ maintained
3255   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
3256   *      -->P_BANFN   PR No.
3257   *      <--P_NO_REQ  No. of RFQ
3258   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
3259   FORM get_no_rfq_maint  USING    p_banfn
3260                          CHANGING p_no_req.
3261     DATA : BEGIN OF wa_ebeln,
3262              banfn TYPE eban-banfn,
3263              ebeln TYPE ekko-ebeln,
3264            END OF wa_ebeln.
3265
3266     DATA : ist_ebeln LIKE TABLE OF wa_ebeln.
3267
3268     DATA : l_count TYPE sy-tabix.
3269
3270     SELECT banfn ebeln FROM m_mekke
3271          INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE ist_ebeln
3272             WHERE banfn =  p_banfn.
3273
3274     IF NOT ist_ebeln[] IS INITIAL.
3275
3276       SORT ist_ebeln BY ebeln.
3277
3278       DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM ist_ebeln  COMPARING ebeln.
3279
3280       SELECT COUNT( * ) FROM ekko INTO l_count
3281            FOR ALL ENTRIES IN ist_ebeln
3282                WHERE ebeln = ist_ebeln-ebeln AND
3283                      statu = 'A'.
3284
3285       IF sy-subrc = 0.
3286         p_no_req = l_count.
3287       ELSE.
3288         CLEAR p_no_req.
3289       ENDIF.
3290
3291     ELSE.
3292
3293       CLEAR p_no_req.
3294
3295     ENDIF.
3296
3297   ENDFORM.                    " GET_NO_RFQ_MAINT
3298
3299   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3300   *&      Form  DISPLAY_NITDATE
3301   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3302   * To set I/O attribute of screen field NIIT Date in Create/Change mode
3303   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
3304   FORM display_nitdate .
3305     IF g_ok_code = 'NEW' OR g_ok_code = 'CHNG'.
3306       IF NOT zmm_pur_tender_d_st-tndr_val IS INITIAL OR
3307         NOT zmm_pur_tender_d_st-tndr_proc IS INITIAL.
3308         IF zmm_pur_tender_d_st-tndr_val < 500000 OR
3309            zmm_pur_tender_d_st-tndr_proc = 10 OR
3310            zmm_pur_tender_d_st-tndr_proc = 11 OR
3311            zmm_pur_tender_d_st-tndr_proc = 12 OR
3312            zmm_pur_tender_d_st-tndr_proc = 13 OR
3313            zmm_pur_tender_d_st-tndr_proc = 14 OR
3314            zmm_pur_tender_d_st-tndr_proc = 15 OR
3315            zmm_pur_tender_d_st-tndr_proc =  7 OR
3316            zmm_pur_tender_d_st-tndr_proc =  8 OR
3317            zmm_pur_tender_d_st-tndr_proc =  9.
3318           LOOP AT SCREEN.
3319             IF screen-name = 'ZMM_PUR_TENDER_D_ST-NITDATE'.
3320               screen-input = 0.
3321               MODIFY SCREEN.
3322               CLEAR zmm_pur_tender_d_st-nitdate.
3323             ENDIF.
3324           ENDLOOP.
3325         ELSE.
3326           LOOP AT SCREEN.
3327             IF screen-name = 'ZMM_PUR_TENDER_D_ST-NITDATE'.
3328               screen-input = 1.
3329               MODIFY SCREEN.
3330             ENDIF.
3331           ENDLOOP.
3332         ENDIF.
3333       ENDIF.
3334     ENDIF.
3335   ENDFORM.                    " DISPLAY_NITDATE

3337   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3338   *&      Form  UPD_PURQ_PROFILE
3339   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3340   * Update Procurement Profile of  Purchase Requistion
3341   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
3342   FORM upd_purq_profile.
3343
3344     DATA: BEGIN OF ist_eban OCCURS 0.
3345             INCLUDE STRUCTURE ueban.
3346     DATA:     upd(1) TYPE c,
3347               del(1) TYPE c,
3348               END OF ist_eban.
3349
3350     DATA : ist_eban_f LIKE TABLE OF ist_eban WITH HEADER LINE.
3351
3352     DATA: pe_t_xeban      LIKE ueban OCCURS 0 WITH HEADER LINE.
3353
3354     DATA: pe_t_xebkn      LIKE uebkn OCCURS 0 WITH HEADER LINE.
3355
3356     DATA: pe_t_yeban   LIKE ueban OCCURS 0 WITH HEADER LINE,
3357           pe_t_yeban_t LIKE ueban OCCURS 0 WITH HEADER LINE.
3358
3359     DATA: pe_t_yebkn      LIKE uebkn OCCURS 0 WITH HEADER LINE.
3360
3361     DATA : BEGIN OF wa_banfn,
3362              banfn TYPE eban-banfn,
3363            END OF wa_banfn.
3364
3365     DATA : ist_banfn   LIKE TABLE OF wa_banfn.
3366
3367     IF zmm_pur_tender_d_st-bsart+0(2) = 'ET' AND
3368        NOT zmm_tms_pb-pr_bid_op_act_dt IS INITIAL.
3369
3370       IF NOT zmm_pur_tender_d_st-banfn IS INITIAL.
3371         MOVE zmm_pur_tender_d_st-banfn TO wa_banfn-banfn.
3372         APPEND wa_banfn TO ist_banfn.
3373       ENDIF.
3374
3375       IF NOT zmm_pur_tender_d_st-banfn2 IS INITIAL.
3376         MOVE zmm_pur_tender_d_st-banfn2 TO wa_banfn-banfn.
3377         APPEND wa_banfn TO ist_banfn.
3378       ENDIF.
3379
3380       IF NOT zmm_pur_tender_d_st-banfn3 IS INITIAL.
3381         MOVE zmm_pur_tender_d_st-banfn3 TO wa_banfn-banfn.
3382         APPEND wa_banfn TO ist_banfn.
3383       ENDIF.
3384
3385       IF NOT zmm_pur_tender_d_st-banfn4 IS INITIAL.
3386         MOVE zmm_pur_tender_d_st-banfn4 TO wa_banfn-banfn.
3387         APPEND wa_banfn TO ist_banfn.
3388       ENDIF.
3389
3390       IF NOT ist_banfn[] IS INITIAL.
3391
3392         LOOP AT ist_banfn INTO wa_banfn.
3393
3394           REFRESH : ist_eban,
3395                     pe_t_yeban_t,
3396                     pe_t_xeban,
3397                     pe_t_xebkn,
3398                     pe_t_yeban,
3399                     pe_t_yebkn.
3400
3401           CLEAR : ist_eban,
3402                   pe_t_yeban_t,
3403                   pe_t_xeban,
3404                   pe_t_xebkn,
3405                   pe_t_yeban,
3406                   pe_t_yebkn.
3407
3408           SELECT * FROM eban
3409              INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE ist_eban
3410                    WHERE banfn = wa_banfn-banfn.
3411
3412           SORT ist_eban BY banfn bnfpo.
3413
3414           LOOP AT ist_eban.
3415
3416             MOVE-CORRESPONDING ist_eban TO pe_t_yeban_t.
3417
3418             APPEND pe_t_yeban_t.
3419
3420           ENDLOOP.
3421
3422           LOOP AT ist_eban.
3423
3424             CLEAR : ist_eban-eprofile.
3425
3426             ist_eban-kz = 'U'.
3427
3428             MODIFY ist_eban INDEX sy-tabix TRANSPORTING eprofile kz.
3429
3430           ENDLOOP.
3431
3432           LOOP AT ist_eban.
3433
3434             LOOP AT pe_t_yeban_t WHERE banfn = ist_eban-banfn AND
3435                                        bnfpo = ist_eban-bnfpo.
3436
3437               MOVE-CORRESPONDING ist_eban TO pe_t_xeban.
3438
3439               APPEND pe_t_xeban.
3440
3441               MOVE-CORRESPONDING pe_t_yeban_t TO pe_t_yeban.
3442
3443               APPEND pe_t_yeban.
3444
3445             ENDLOOP.
3446
3447           ENDLOOP.
3448
3449           CALL FUNCTION 'Z_ME_CREATE_REQ'
3450             TABLES
3451               xeban = pe_t_xeban.
3452
3453           CALL FUNCTION 'ME_UPDATE_REQUISITION'
3454             TABLES
3455               xeban = pe_t_xeban
3456               xebkn = pe_t_xebkn
3457               yeban = pe_t_yeban
3458               yebkn = pe_t_yebkn.
3459
3460         ENDLOOP.
3461
3462       ENDIF.
3463
3464     ENDIF.
3465
3466   ENDFORM.                    "UPD_PURQ_PROFILE
